# Skills · R · part 37

856 entries, rfs-empirical-design to risk-mitigation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **rfs-empirical-design**: Use when sample construction, estimator choice, factor/portfolio design, or measurement is the bottleneck for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript. Settles design choices that make the identification credible; does NOT pick the identification strategy or run robustness. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-empirical-design) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-identification**: Use when the causal-inference or asset-pricing identification strategy is the bottleneck for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript — quasi-experiments (DID, IV, RDD, event study) and factor-model identification. Stress-tests the design before drafting tables. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-identification) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-internet-appendix**: Use when deciding what belongs in the journal-hosted Internet Appendix vs. the main paper for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript, and how to structure it. Organizes supplementary material; does NOT generate the robustness content itself. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-internet-appendix) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-literature-positioning**: Use when the related-work framing is the bottleneck for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript — defining the precise delta against JF/JFE/RFS and prior literature. Builds positioning, not the empirical design or the topic itself. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-literature-positioning) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-rebuttal**: Use after a decision letter (R&R or reject-and-resubmit) on a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript, to structure the response letter and revision plan. Writes the rebuttal; assumes the manuscript itself is being revised in parallel. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-rebuttal) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-referee-strategy**: Use when choosing suggested/opposed referees or running an objection pre-mortem before submitting a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript. Anticipates reviewer concerns; does NOT write the rebuttal (that is rfs-rebuttal). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-referee-strategy) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-robustness**: Use when results may be fragile or when multiple-testing / out-of-sample discipline is the bottleneck for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript. Builds the robustness battery referees will demand; does NOT design identification or write the rebuttal. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-robustness) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript — the SFS Editorial Express portal, cover letter, formatting, Internet Appendix, submission fee, and SFS norms. Checks readiness; does NOT write the paper or the rebuttal. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-tables-figures**: Use when main-paper exhibits are the bottleneck for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript — table layout, standard-error reporting, and publication-grade figures. Finalizes exhibits; does NOT decide robustness content or move material to the appendix. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-tables-figures) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-topic-selection**: Use when the research question or contribution is the bottleneck for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript. Pressure-tests novelty-plus-rigor fit and drafts the contribution claim; does NOT design the empirics or position the literature in detail. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Review-of-Financial-Studies-Skills/skills/rfs-topic-selection) · ★1.1k)
- **rfs-workflow**: Use when deciding which rfs-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing manuscript work from topic selection through rebuttal for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript. - **rfs-writing-style**: Use as a late-stage polish when prose, contribution framing, or the abstract/introduction is the bottleneck for a The Review of Financial Studies (RFS) manuscript. - **riscv-migrate**: x86/ARM → RISC-V（含 RVV）一体化迁移技能，以性能最大化为默认目标。采用「主 Agent 编排 + 阶段内动态 Subagent」架构：主 Agent 是唯一编排者与状态提交者，Subagent 承担阶段内工作单元。按四大阶段推进：一、扫描迁移点（含纯 C 可向量化热点识别，可并行扫描 Subagent）；二、按迁移点分流迁移（规划 Subagent 先产出互不重叠任务分组 → 迁移 Subagent 先做必要性分析再迁移 → 独立组可并行；汇编/RVV/AutoVec 条目必过 Reviewer 门禁 + 快速 mca 性能门禁）；三、工程级编译修复 + 向量化审计（并行诊断、串行修复）；四、性能分析与优化（对全部 asm/RVV/AutoVec 条目默认执行，多热点并行 llvm-mca，A/B 择优）。每个迁移点的状态以 `scan_result.json` 条目里的 `status`（TODO/START/DONE）+ `marking`（异常/性能说明）+ `perf`（结构化性能数据）字段同步，权威源在 JSON，不在源码注释。**只有主 Agent 可以写入 `scan_result.json`**。 ([Bonn12138/riscv-everything](https://github.com/Bonn12138/riscv-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/riscv-migrate))
